      (function () {
        var forcedTheme = null;
        function applyTheme(el) {
          try {
            var theme = forcedTheme;
            if (!theme) {
              var stored = localStorage.getItem("theme");
              theme = stored === "light" || stored === "dark" ? stored : (window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)").matches ? "dark" : "light");
            }
            if (!el.classList.contains(theme)) {
              el.classList.remove("light", "dark");
              el.classList.add(theme);
            }
            if (el.style.colorScheme !== theme) {
              el.style.colorScheme = theme;
            }
          } catch (e) {
          }
        }
        // Chrome regions (header / sidebar) whose configured background contrasts with
        // the page theme carry data-light-theme-class / data-dark-theme-class; give each
        // the class for the root's current theme so its color vars re-scope with it.
        function scopeChrome(el) {
          try {
            if (!el.querySelectorAll) return;
            var dark = el.classList.contains("dark");
            var nodes = el.querySelectorAll("[data-light-theme-class],[data-dark-theme-class]");
            for (var i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
              var node = nodes[i];
              var want = node.getAttribute(dark ? "data-dark-theme-class" : "data-light-theme-class");
              node.classList.remove("light", "dark");
              if (want) node.classList.add(want);
            }
          } catch (e) {
          }
        }
        // Prepare the incoming root before Astro swaps it; page-load runs after paint.
        document.addEventListener("astro:before-swap", function (event) {
          applyTheme(event.newDocument.documentElement);
          scopeChrome(event.newDocument.documentElement);
        });
        applyTheme(document.documentElement);
        if (document.body) {
          scopeChrome(document.documentElement);
        } else {
          document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () { scopeChrome(document.documentElement); });
        }
        // The theme switcher (next-themes) flips the root class after load.
        if (typeof MutationObserver === "function") {
          var lastDark = document.documentElement.classList.contains("dark");
          new MutationObserver(function () {
            var dark = document.documentElement.classList.contains("dark");
            if (dark === lastDark) return;
            lastDark = dark;
            scopeChrome(document.documentElement);
          })
            .observe(document.documentElement, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ["class"] });
        }
      })();

      (function () {
        // Only write when the value changes: every write restyles the whole
        // document. The ResizeObserver path reads `contentRect` after layout
        // instead of forcing a synchronous layout before first paint.
        function applyHeaderHeight(height) {
          var value = height + "px";
          if (document.documentElement.style.getPropertyValue("--header-height") !== value) {
            document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--header-height", value);
          }
        }
        function syncHeaderHeight() {
          var header = document.getElementById("fern-header");
          if (header) {
            var style = window.getComputedStyle(header);
            applyHeaderHeight(header.clientHeight - parseFloat(style.paddingTop) - parseFloat(style.paddingBottom));
          }
        }
        var ro;
        function setup() {
          var header = document.getElementById("fern-header");
          if (header && "ResizeObserver" in window) {
            if (ro) { ro.disconnect(); }
            ro = new ResizeObserver(function (entries) {
              var entry = entries[entries.length - 1];
              if (entry) { applyHeaderHeight(entry.contentRect.height); }
            });
            ro.observe(header);
          } else {
            (window.requestAnimationFrame || window.setTimeout)(syncHeaderHeight);
          }
        }
        setup();
        if (!window.__fernHeaderHeightBound) {
          window.__fernHeaderHeightBound = true;
          document.addEventListener("astro:page-load", setup);
          // The swap replaces <html>'s attributes (including the inline style
          // holding the synced value), so carry it over to the incoming document
          // or the header/main/sidebar briefly snap to the stylesheet fallback.
          document.addEventListener("astro:before-swap", function (event) {
            var current = document.documentElement.style.getPropertyValue("--header-height");
            if (current && event.newDocument) {
              event.newDocument.documentElement.style.setProperty("--header-height", current);
            }
          });
        }
      })();
    </script><!-- Main (matches MainElement) --><main class="fern-main relative z-0 flex transition-all duration-500 ease-out mt-(--header-height)" data-theme="default" data-fern-style="transition: max-width 500ms ease-out"><a href="#fern-sidebar" class="sr-only max-lg:hidden focus:not-sr-only focus:absolute focus:left-4 focus:top-4 focus:z-50 focus:rounded-md focus:border focus:border-border-concealed focus:bg-(color:--background) focus:px-4 focus:py-2 focus:text-body focus:no-underline focus:shadow-md">Skip to navigation</a><aside id="fern-sidebar-spacer" data-state="fixed"></aside><!-- Page content is an Astro slot, so the shared layout wrappers are mirrored here while their stateful components are reused directly. --><div hidden aria-hidden="true" data-fern-agent-context>This page is for version v2 API (default).

class="fern-layout-page"><section class="fern-changelog-content" data-has-aside="false"><div class="max-w-content-width mx-auto w-full"><header class="my-8 space-y-2"><div class="flex flex-row href="/v2/changelog"><h1 class="fern-page-heading text-balance wrap-anywhere">Release Notes</h1></a></div></div><div class="hidden md:flex"><button tabindex="0" data-state="off" aria-disabled="false" class="fern-rss-feed-button fern-button outlined normal rounded"><span class="fern-button-content"><span class="fern-button-text">Subscribe via RSS</span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" max-w-none break-words"><div data-fern-style="display:contents"></div></div></div></section><article class="fern-changelog-content mt-8" id="2026-09-30T00:00:00.000Z" data-has-aside="true"><aside><div class="fern-changelog-label"><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/embed-v5" class="fern-docs-badge large grayscale subtle">September 30, 2026</a><div class="filter-row"></div></div></aside><div class="max-w-content-width mx-auto w-full"><div class="eyebrow"><div class="fern-changelog-label"><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/embed-v5" class="fern-docs-badge large grayscale subtle">September 30, 2026</a><div class="filter-row"></div></div></div><div class="fern-prose prose max-w-none break-words"><h2><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/embed-v5" class="not-prose">Announcing Cohere&#x27;s Embed 5 Models</a></h2><div data-fern-style="display:contents"><p>We’re pleased to announce the release of <a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v2/docs/cohere-embed">Embed 5</a>, Cohere’s most powerful embeddings family yet.</p>
<p>Embed 5 delivers frontier retrieval quality on complex enterprise data, with major gains over Embed 4 on visually rich documents, financial filings, parsed PDFs, code, and multilingual retrieval.</p>
<h2 id="2026-09-30-key-features" class="max-md:pr-4" data-state="closed" data-fern-style="cursor:pointer">Key features</h2>
<ul class="[&amp;&gt;li]:relative [&amp;&gt;li]:before:text-(color:--grayscale-a10) mb-3 list-none pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:before:absolute [&amp;&gt;li]:before:ml-[-22px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:mt-[-1px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:content-[&#x27;⦁&#x27;] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:self-center">
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Two model variants available:</strong>
<ul class="[&amp;&gt;li]:relative [&amp;&gt;li]:before:text-(color:--grayscale-a10) mb-3 list-none pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:before:absolute [&amp;&gt;li]:before:ml-[-22px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:mt-[-1px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:content-[&#x27;⦁&#x27;] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:self-center">
<li class=""><code>embed-v5.0-pro</code>: Optimized for the highest retrieval quality, particularly for offline indexing and quality-critical retrieval</li>
<li class=""><code>embed-v5.0-fast</code>: Optimized for low latency and high throughput, particularly for interactive search, agent loops, and high-volume query traffic</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Shared embedding space</strong>: Pro and Fast share an embedding space, so a corpus indexed with one model can be queried with the other. We recommend indexing with Pro and querying with Fast.</li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Multimodal inputs</strong>: Embed text, images, and mixed text-and-image inputs (e.g. PDF pages) in a single vector</li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Multilingual support</strong>: Supports over 100 languages</li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Extended context length</strong>: 128k token context window</li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Flexible storage</strong>: Matryoshka embeddings in the following dimensions: <code>[256, 512, 768, 1024, 1536, 2048]</code>, with <code>float</code>, <code>int8</code>, and <code>binary</code> output types</li>
</ul>
<h2 id="2026-09-30-availability" class="max-md:pr-4" data-state="closed" data-fern-style="cursor:pointer">Availability</h2>
<p>Embed 5 is available through the <a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v2/reference/embed">Embed API</a>, as well as Microsoft Foundry
(<a class="fern-mdx-link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" href="https://ai.azure.com/catalog/models/Cohere-Embed-V5-Pro">Pro<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon"><path d="M15 3h6v6"></path><path d="M10 14 21 3"></path><path d="M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6"></path></svg></a>,
<a class="fern-mdx-link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" href="https://ai.azure.com/catalog/models/Cohere-Embed-V5-Fast">Fast<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon"><path d="M15 3h6v6"></path><path d="M10 14 21 3"></path><path d="M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6"></path></svg></a>) and Amazon SageMaker
(<a class="fern-mdx-link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" href="https://aws.amazon.com/marketplace/pp/prodview-pobc74bcuaohi">Pro<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon"><path d="M15 3h6v6"></path><path d="M10 14 21 3"></path><path d="M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6"></path></svg></a>,
<a class="fern-mdx-link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" href="https://aws.amazon.com/marketplace/pp/prodview-qffayhd7sgkja">Fast<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon"><path d="M15 3h6v6"></path><path d="M10 14 21 3"></path><path d="M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6"></path></svg></a>).
For single-tenant deployment, Embed 5 is also available in Cohere’s <a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v2/docs/model-vault">Model Vault</a>.</p>
<p>For more details, see the <a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v2/docs/cohere-embed">model documentation</a>.</p></div></div></div></article><hr class="my-4"/><article class="fern-changelog-content mt-16" id="2026-09-09T00:00:00.000Z" data-has-aside="true"><aside><div class="fern-changelog-label"><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/north-small-translate-1-0" class="fern-docs-badge large grayscale subtle">September 9, 2026</a><div class="filter-row"></div></div></aside><div class="max-w-content-width mx-auto w-full"><div class="eyebrow"><div class="fern-changelog-label"><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/north-small-translate-1-0" class="fern-docs-badge large grayscale subtle">September 9, 2026</a><div class="filter-row"></div></div></div><div class="fern-prose prose max-w-none break-words"><h2><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/north-small-translate-1-0" class="not-prose">Announcing Cohere&#x27;s North Small Translate</a></h2><div data-fern-style="display:contents"><p>We’re pleased to announce the release of
<a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v2/docs/north-small-translate-1.0">North Small Translate</a>, an open-weights mixture-of-experts model purpose-built
for machine translation across more than 50 languages.</p>
<p>North Small Translate is designed to give researchers, developers, and enterprises flexible ways to evaluate and
deploy machine translation while retaining control over their data and infrastructure.</p>
<h2 id="2026-09-09-key-features" class="max-md:pr-4" data-state="closed" data-fern-style="cursor:pointer">Key features</h2>
<ul class="[&amp;&gt;li]:relative [&amp;&gt;li]:before:text-(color:--grayscale-a10) mb-3 list-none pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:before:absolute [&amp;&gt;li]:before:ml-[-22px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:mt-[-1px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:content-[&#x27;⦁&#x27;] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:self-center">
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Purpose-built translation</strong>: Optimized for machine translation across more than 50 languages and locale
variants.</li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Efficient MoE architecture</strong>: 218 billion total parameters with 25 billion active parameters.</li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Flexible deployment</strong>: Available through the free-tier Chat V2 API and as open weights in W4A16, FP8, and BF16
for non-commercial use.</li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Private deployment</strong>: Suggested deployment hardware by quantization format:<!-- -->
<ul class="[&amp;&gt;li]:relative [&amp;&gt;li]:before:text-(color:--grayscale-a10) mb-3 list-none pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:before:absolute [&amp;&gt;li]:before:ml-[-22px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:mt-[-1px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:content-[&#x27;⦁&#x27;] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:self-center">
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">W4A16</strong>: Two H100s or one B200</li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">FP8</strong>: Four H100s or two B200s</li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">BF16</strong>: Eight H100s or four B200s</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<h2 id="2026-09-09-technical-details" class="max-md:pr-4" data-state="closed" data-fern-style="cursor:pointer">Technical details</h2>
<ul class="[&amp;&gt;li]:relative [&amp;&gt;li]:before:text-(color:--grayscale-a10) mb-3 list-none pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:before:absolute [&amp;&gt;li]:before:ml-[-22px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:mt-[-1px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:content-[&#x27;⦁&#x27;] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:self-center">
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Model name</strong>: <code>north-small-translate-1-0</code></li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Context length</strong>: 16K</li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">License</strong>:
<a class="fern-mdx-link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" href="https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-nc/4.0/">Creative Commons Attribution-NonCommercial 4.0<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon"><path d="M15 3h6v6"></path><path d="M10 14 21 3"></path><path d="M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6"></path></svg></a></li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Open-weights formats</strong>: W4A16, FP8, and BF16</li>
</ul>
<h2 id="2026-09-09-availability" class="max-md:pr-4" data-state="closed" data-fern-style="cursor:pointer">Availability</h2>
<p>North Small Translate is available on the free tier through the <a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v2/reference/chat">Chat V2 API</a>. Open weights are
available in W4A16, FP8, and BF16 on <a class="fern-mdx-link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" href="https://huggingface.co/CohereLabs/North-Small-Translate-1.0">Hugging Face<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon"><path d="M15 3h6v6"></path><path d="M10 14 21 3"></path><path d="M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6"></path></svg></a>
for non-commercial use under the CC BY-NC 4.0 license.</p>
<p>For supported languages, use cases, and an API example, see the
<a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v2/docs/north-small-translate-1.0">model documentation</a>.</p></div></div></div></article><hr class="my-4"/><article class="fern-changelog-content mt-16" id="2026-08-27T00:00:00.000Z" data-has-aside="true"><aside><div class="fern-changelog-label"><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/parse" class="fern-docs-badge large grayscale subtle">August 27, 2026</a><div class="filter-row"></div></div></aside><div class="max-w-content-width mx-auto w-full"><div class="eyebrow"><div class="fern-changelog-label"><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/parse" class="fern-docs-badge large grayscale subtle">August 27, 2026</a><div class="filter-row"></div></div></div><div class="fern-prose prose max-w-none break-words"><h2><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/parse" class="not-prose">Meet Cohere Parse</a></h2><div data-fern-style="display:contents"><p>Today we are releasing Cohere <a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v2/docs/parse">Parse</a>.</p>
<p>Parse (model ID: <code>parse-v5.0</code>) turns complex documents into clean, structured Markdown ready for downstream AI workflows. The 2.3B-parameter multimodal model extracts text in reading order, tables, lists, forms, images and captions, page boundaries, and visual element locations.</p>
<p>Outputs include Markdown/HTML content, HTML-formatted tables, bounding boxes, and image descriptions — preserving both document structure and layout for easier rendering and processing.</p>
<p>Key specs: 8K context window · ~4.6GB model size · Markdown output</p>
<h2 id="2026-08-27-availability" class="max-md:pr-4" data-state="closed" data-fern-style="cursor:pointer">Availability</h2>
<p>Cohere Parse is available through the Parse API, as well as <a class="fern-mdx-link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" href="https://ai.azure.com/catalog/models/Cohere-parse-v5">Microsoft Foundry<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon"><path d="M15 3h6v6"></path><path d="M10 14 21 3"></path><path d="M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6"></path></svg></a> and <a class="fern-mdx-link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" href="https://aws.amazon.com/marketplace/pp/prodview-25vdn5x53zqgo">AWS SageMaker<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon"><path d="M15 3h6v6"></path><path d="M10 14 21 3"></path><path d="M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6"></path></svg></a>.
For single-tenant deployment, Parse is also available in <a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v2/docs/model-vault">Model Vault</a>.</p>
<p>For more details, see the <a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v2/docs/parse">model documentation</a>.</p></div></div></div></article><hr class="my-4"/><article class="fern-changelog-content mt-16" id="2026-07-07T00:00:00.000Z" data-has-aside="true"><aside><div class="fern-changelog-label"><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/transcribe-arabic" class="fern-docs-badge large grayscale subtle">July 7, 2026</a><div class="filter-row"></div></div></aside><div class="max-w-content-width mx-auto w-full"><div class="eyebrow"><div class="fern-changelog-label"><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/transcribe-arabic" class="fern-docs-badge large grayscale subtle">July 7, 2026</a><div class="filter-row"></div></div></div><div class="fern-prose prose max-w-none break-words"><h2><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/transcribe-arabic" class="not-prose">Meet Cohere Transcribe Arabic</a></h2><div data-fern-style="display:contents"><p>Today we are releasing <a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v2/docs/transcribe-arabic">Cohere Transcribe Arabic</a>.</p>
<p>This open-source speech-to-text model is a fine-tune of Cohere Transcribe using Arabic speech data. It
lets Arabic speakers transcribe their voice with unmatched accuracy and support for regional dialects or
speech patterns.</p>
<p>It is currently the most accurate open-source Arabic ASR model available today and is optimized for
production inference and throughput.</p>
<h2 id="2026-07-07-technical-details" class="max-md:pr-4" data-state="closed" data-fern-style="cursor:pointer">Technical Details</h2>
<ul class="[&amp;&gt;li]:relative [&amp;&gt;li]:before:text-(color:--grayscale-a10) mb-3 list-none pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:before:absolute [&amp;&gt;li]:before:ml-[-22px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:mt-[-1px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:content-[&#x27;⦁&#x27;] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:self-center">
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Model Name</strong>: <code>cohere-transcribe-arabic-07-2026</code></li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Size</strong>: 2B</li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Architecture</strong>: conformer-based encoder-decoder</li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Languages supported</strong>: Arabic (all major dialects), English (including English spoken with an
Arabic accent)</li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">License</strong>: <a class="fern-mdx-link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" href="https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0">Apache 2.0<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon"><path d="M15 3h6v6"></path><path d="M10 14 21 3"></path><path d="M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6"></path></svg></a></li>
</ul>
<h2 id="2026-07-07-availability" class="max-md:pr-4" data-state="closed" data-fern-style="cursor:pointer">Availability</h2>
<p>Cohere Transcribe Arabic is available through the V2 Audio Transcriptions API and as open weights on
<a class="fern-mdx-link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" href="https://huggingface.co/CohereLabs/cohere-transcribe-arabic-06-2026">Hugging Face<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon"><path d="M15 3h6v6"></path><path d="M10 14 21 3"></path><path d="M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6"></path></svg></a>. For
production use, <a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v2/docs/model-vault">Model Vault</a> deployment is also supported.</p>
<p>For more details, see the <a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v2/docs/transcribe-arabic">model documentation</a>.</p></div></div></div></article><hr class="my-4"/><article class="fern-changelog-content mt-16" id="2026-06-09T00:00:00.000Z" data-has-aside="true"><aside><div class="fern-changelog-label"><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/north-mini-code-1-0" class="fern-docs-badge large grayscale subtle">June 9, 2026</a><div class="filter-row"></div></div></aside><div class="max-w-content-width mx-auto w-full"><div class="eyebrow"><div class="fern-changelog-label"><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/north-mini-code-1-0" class="fern-docs-badge large grayscale subtle">June 9, 2026</a><div class="filter-row"></div></div></div><div class="fern-prose prose max-w-none break-words"><h2><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/north-mini-code-1-0" class="not-prose">Announcing Cohere&#x27;s North Mini Code</a></h2><div data-fern-style="display:contents"><p>We’re pleased to announce the release of <a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v2/docs/north-mini-code-1.0">North Mini Code</a>, Cohere’s first
agentic coding model. It is a 30 billion total / 3 billion active parameter Mixture of Experts model
trained specifically for agentic coding, with a small enough active footprint to run on local hardware.</p>
<h2 id="2026-06-09-technical-details" class="max-md:pr-4" data-state="closed" data-fern-style="cursor:pointer">Technical Details</h2>
<ul class="[&amp;&gt;li]:relative [&amp;&gt;li]:before:text-(color:--grayscale-a10) mb-3 list-none pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:before:absolute [&amp;&gt;li]:before:ml-[-22px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:mt-[-1px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:content-[&#x27;⦁&#x27;] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:self-center">
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Model Name</strong>: <code>north-mini-code-1-0</code></li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Context Length</strong>: 256K input, 64K output</li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">License</strong>: <a class="fern-mdx-link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" href="https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0">Apache 2.0<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon"><path d="M15 3h6v6"></path><path d="M10 14 21 3"></path><path d="M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6"></path></svg></a></li>

<h2 id="2026-06-09-availability" class="max-md:pr-4" data-state="closed" data-fern-style="cursor:pointer">Availability</h2>

production use, <a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v2/docs/model-vault">Model Vault</a> deployment is also supported.</p>
<p>For more details, see the <a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v2/docs/north-mini-code-1.0">model documentation</a>.</p></div></div></div></article><hr class="my-4"/><article class="fern-changelog-content mt-16" id="2026-05-20T00:00:00.000Z" data-has-aside="true"><aside><div class="fern-changelog-label"><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/command-a-plus-05-2026" class="fern-docs-badge large grayscale subtle">May 20, 2026</a><div class="filter-row"></div></div></aside><div class="max-w-content-width mx-auto w-full"><div class="eyebrow"><div class="fern-changelog-label"><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/command-a-plus-05-2026" class="fern-docs-badge large grayscale subtle">May 20, 2026</a><div class="filter-row"></div></div></div><div class="fern-prose prose max-w-none break-words"><h2><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/command-a-plus-05-2026" class="not-prose">Announcing Cohere&#x27;s Command A+</a></h2><div data-fern-style="display:contents"><p>We’re pleased to announce the release of <a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v2/docs/command-a-plus">Command A+</a>, the last model in the Command A
family of models, combining support for vision inputs, reasoning capabilities, translation capabilities, and
agentic tasks all within the same model. It is also notably our first Mixture of Experts (MoE) model with 25
billion active parameters ands 218 billion total parameters.</p>
<h2 id="2026-05-20-key-features" class="max-md:pr-4" data-state="closed" data-fern-style="cursor:pointer">Key Features</h2>
<ul class="[&amp;&gt;li]:relative [&amp;&gt;li]:before:text-(color:--grayscale-a10) mb-3 list-none pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:before:absolute [&amp;&gt;li]:before:ml-[-22px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:mt-[-1px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:content-[&#x27;⦁&#x27;] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:self-center">
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Agentic Applications</strong>: With notable performance increases in tool use and agentic tasks, Command A+ is
the strongest agentic model in the Command family.</li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Expanded Multilingual Support</strong>: With 48 languages supported, including all official EU languages, this
more than doubles the support of languages from our prior models.</li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Efficient &amp; Fast</strong>: With as few as 1 x B200 or 2 x H100s required to deploy the model, and up to 110%
throughput increase and 30% decrease in latency over Command A Reasoning, the model is designed for
production-grade deployments.</li>
</ul>
<h2 id="2026-05-20-technical-details" class="max-md:pr-4" data-state="closed" data-fern-style="cursor:pointer">Technical Details</h2>
<ul class="[&amp;&gt;li]:relative [&amp;&gt;li]:before:text-(color:--grayscale-a10) mb-3 list-none pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:before:absolute [&amp;&gt;li]:before:ml-[-22px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:mt-[-1px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:content-[&#x27;⦁&#x27;] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:self-center">
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Model Name</strong>: command-a-plus-05-2026</li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Context Length</strong>: 128K input, 64K output</li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Languages covered</strong>: English, Arabic, Bulgarian, Bengali, Catalan, Czech, Danish, German, Greek, Spanish, Estonian, Persian, Finnish, Filipino, French, Irish, Hebrew, Hindi, Croatian, Hungarian, Indonesian, Icelandic, Italian, Japanese, Korean, Lithuanian, Latvian, Malay, Maltese, Dutch, Norwegian, Punjabi, Polish, Portuguese, Romanian, Russian, Slovak, Slovenian, Serbian, Swedish, Tamil, Telugu, Thai, Turkish, Ukrainian, Urdu, Vietnamese, Chinese.</li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">License</strong>: <a class="fern-mdx-link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" href="https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0">Apache 2.0<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon"><path d="M15 3h6v6"></path><path d="M10 14 21 3"></path><path d="M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6"></path></svg></a></li>
</ul>
<h2 id="2026-05-20-availability" class="max-md:pr-4" data-state="closed" data-fern-style="cursor:pointer">Availability</h2>
<p>Command A+ (<code>command-a-plus-05-2026</code>) is now available for all Cohere users through our standard API
endpoints. For enterprise customers, <a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v2/docs/private-deployment-overview">private deployment</a> options are
available to ensure maximum security and control over your translation workflows.</p>
<p>For more detailed information about Command A+, including technical specifications and implementation
examples, visit our <a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v2/docs/command-a-plus">model documentation</a>.</p></div></div></div></article><hr class="my-4"/><article class="fern-changelog-content mt-16" id="2026-04-04T00:00:00.000Z" data-has-aside="true"><aside><div class="fern-changelog-label"><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/2026-04-04-embed-v2-aya-8b-retirement" class="fern-docs-badge large grayscale subtle">April 4, 2026</a><div class="filter-row"></div></div></aside><div class="max-w-content-width mx-auto w-full"><div class="eyebrow"><div class="fern-changelog-label"><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/2026-04-04-embed-v2-aya-8b-retirement" class="fern-docs-badge large grayscale subtle">April 4, 2026</a><div class="filter-row"></div></div></div><div class="fern-prose prose max-w-none break-words"><h2><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/2026-04-04-embed-v2-aya-8b-retirement" class="not-prose">Retirement of Embed v2.0 and Aya Expanse / Vision 8B</a></h2><div data-fern-style="display:contents"><h2 id="2026-04-04-retirement-notice" class="max-md:pr-4" data-state="closed" data-fern-style="cursor:pointer">Retirement notice</h2>

</ul>
<p>For the full announcement and lifecycle context, see the <a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v2/docs/deprecations">Deprecations</a> page. For questions or
assistance, contact <a class="fern-mdx-link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" href="mailto:support@cohere.com">support@cohere.com<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon"><path d="M15 3h6v6"></path><path d="M10 14 21 3"></path><path d="M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6"></path></svg></a>.</p></div></div></div></article><hr class="my-4"/><article class="fern-changelog-content mt-16" id="2026-03-26T00:00:00.000Z" data-has-aside="true"><aside><div class="fern-changelog-label"><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/cohere-transcribe-03-2026" class="fern-docs-badge large grayscale subtle">March 26, 2026</a><div class="filter-row"></div></div></aside><div class="max-w-content-width mx-auto w-full"><div class="eyebrow"><div class="fern-changelog-label"><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/cohere-transcribe-03-2026" class="fern-docs-badge large grayscale subtle">March 26, 2026</a><div class="filter-row"></div></div></div><div class="fern-prose prose max-w-none break-words"><h2><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/cohere-transcribe-03-2026" class="not-prose">Announcing the Cohere Transcribe model</a></h2><div data-fern-style="display:contents"><p>We’re pleased to announce the release of <a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v2/docs/transcribe">Cohere Transcribe</a>, our first transcription model.
Cohere Transcribe specializes in audio-in, text-out, automatic speech recognition (ASR).</p>
<h2 id="2026-03-26-technical-details" class="max-md:pr-4" data-state="closed" data-fern-style="cursor:pointer">Technical details</h2>
<ul class="[&amp;&gt;li]:relative [&amp;&gt;li]:before:text-(color:--grayscale-a10) mb-3 list-none pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:before:absolute [&amp;&gt;li]:before:ml-[-22px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:mt-[-1px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:content-[&#x27;⦁&#x27;] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:self-center">
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Model name</strong>: <code>cohere-transcribe-03-2026</code></li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Input</strong>: Audio waveform</li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Output</strong>: Text</li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Languages covered</strong>: English, German, French, Italian, Spanish, Portuguese, Greek, Dutch, Polish,
Vietnamese, Chinese, Arabic, Japanese, Korean.</li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">License</strong>: <a class="fern-mdx-link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" href="https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0">Apache 2.0<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon"><path d="M15 3h6v6"></path><path d="M10 14 21 3"></path><path d="M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6"></path></svg></a></li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">API endpoint</strong>: <a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v2/reference/create-audio-transcription">Audio Transcriptions API</a></li>
</ul>
<h2 id="2026-03-26-getting-started" class="max-md:pr-4" data-state="closed" data-fern-style="cursor:pointer">Getting started</h2>
<p>The model is available immediately through Cohere’s <a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v2/reference/create-audio-transcription">Audio Transcriptions API endpoint</a>.
You can start transcribing audio using the following example query:</p>

<h2 id="2026-03-26-availability" class="max-md:pr-4" data-state="closed" data-fern-style="cursor:pointer">Availability</h2>
<p>You can access Cohere Transcribe via our <a class="fern-mdx-link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" href="http://dashboard.cohere.com/">API<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon"><path d="M15 3h6v6"></path><path d="M10 14 21 3"></path><path d="M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6"></path></svg></a> for free, low-setup experimentation
subject to rate limits. See the <a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v2/docs/rate-limits">Different Types of API Keys and Rate Limits</a> page for
usage details and integration guidance.</p>
<p>For production deployment without rate limits, provision a dedicated <a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v2/docs/model-vault">Model Vault</a>.
This enables low-latency, private cloud inference without having to manage infrastructure. Pricing is
calculated per hour-instance, with discounted plans for longer-term commitments.
<a class="fern-mdx-link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" href="https://cohere.com/contact-sales">Contact our team<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon"><path d="M15 3h6v6"></path><path d="M10 14 21 3"></path><path d="M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6"></path></svg></a> to discuss your requirements.</p></div></div></div></article><hr class="my-4"/><article class="fern-changelog-content mt-16" id="2025-12-11T00:00:00.000Z" data-has-aside="true"><aside><div class="fern-changelog-label"><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/rerank-v4.0" class="fern-docs-badge large grayscale subtle">December 11, 2025</a><div class="filter-row"></div></div></aside><div class="max-w-content-width mx-auto w-full"><div class="eyebrow"><div class="fern-changelog-label"><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/rerank-v4.0" class="fern-docs-badge large grayscale subtle">December 11, 2025</a><div class="filter-row"></div></div></div><div class="fern-prose prose max-w-none break-words"><h2><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/rerank-v4.0" class="not-prose">Cohere&#x27;s Rerank v4.0 Model is Here!</a></h2><div data-fern-style="display:contents"><p>We’re pleased to announce the release of <a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v2/docs/rerank">Rerank 4.0</a> our newest and most performant foundational model for ranking.</p>
<h2 id="2025-12-11-technical-details" class="max-md:pr-4" data-state="closed" data-fern-style="cursor:pointer">Technical Details</h2>
<ul class="[&amp;&gt;li]:relative [&amp;&gt;li]:before:text-(color:--grayscale-a10) mb-3 list-none pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:before:absolute [&amp;&gt;li]:before:ml-[-22px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:mt-[-1px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:content-[&#x27;⦁&#x27;] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:self-center">
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Two model variants available:</strong>
<ul class="[&amp;&gt;li]:relative [&amp;&gt;li]:before:text-(color:--grayscale-a10) mb-3 list-none pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:before:absolute [&amp;&gt;li]:before:ml-[-22px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:mt-[-1px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:content-[&#x27;⦁&#x27;] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:self-center">
<li class=""><code>rerank-v4.0-pro</code>: Optimized for state-of-the-art quality and complex use-cases</li>
<li class=""><code>rerank-v4.0-fast</code>: Optimized for low latency and high throughput use-cases</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Multilingual support</strong>: Re-rank both English and non-English documents</li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Semi-structured data support</strong>: Re-rank JSON documents</li>
<li class=""><strong class="font-semibold">Extended context length</strong>: 32k token context window</li>
</ul>

class="fern-changelog-content mt-16" id="2025-09-16T00:00:00.000Z" data-has-aside="true"><aside><div class="fern-changelog-label"><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/2025-09-15-major-command-deprecations" class="fern-docs-badge large grayscale subtle">September 16, 2025</a><div class="filter-row"></div></div></aside><div class="max-w-content-width mx-auto w-full"><div class="eyebrow"><div class="fern-changelog-label"><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/2025-09-15-major-command-deprecations" class="fern-docs-badge large grayscale subtle">September 16, 2025</a><div class="filter-row"></div></div></div><div class="fern-prose prose max-w-none break-words"><h2><a data-astro-prefetch="viewport" href="/v2/changelog/2025-09-15-major-command-deprecations" class="not-prose">Announcing Major Command Deprecations</a></h2><div data-fern-style="display:contents"><p>As part of our ongoing commitment to delivering advanced AI solutions, we are deprecating the following models, features, and API endpoints:</p>
<p>Deprecated Models:</p>
<ul class="[&amp;&gt;li]:relative [&amp;&gt;li]:before:text-(color:--grayscale-a10) mb-3 list-none pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:before:absolute [&amp;&gt;li]:before:ml-[-22px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:mt-[-1px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:content-[&#x27;⦁&#x27;] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:self-center">
<li class=""><code>command-r-03-2024</code>  (and the alias <code>command-r</code>)</li>
<li class=""><code>command-r-plus-04-2024</code>  (and the alias <code>command-r-plus</code>)</li>
<li class=""><code>command-light</code></li>
<li class=""><code>command</code></li>
<li class=""><code>summarize</code> (Refer to <a class="fern-mdx-link" href="https://docs.cohere.com/docs/summarizing-text#migration-from-summarize-to-chat-endpoint">the migration guide</a> for alternatives).</li>
</ul>
<p>For command model replacements, we recommend you use <code>command-r-08-2024</code>, <code>command-r-plus-08-2024</code>, or <code>command-a-03-2025</code> (which is the strongest-performing model across domains) instead.</p>
<p>Retired Fine-Tuning Capabilities:
All fine-tuning options via dashboard and API for models including <code>command-light</code>, <code>command</code>, <code>command-r</code>, <code>classify</code>, and <code>rerank</code> are being retired. Previously fine-tuned models will no longer be accessible.</p>
<p>Deprecated Features and API Endpoints:</p>
<ul class="[&amp;&gt;li]:relative [&amp;&gt;li]:before:text-(color:--grayscale-a10) mb-3 list-none pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:pl-3 [&amp;&gt;li]:before:absolute [&amp;&gt;li]:before:ml-[-22px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:mt-[-1px] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:content-[&#x27;⦁&#x27;] [&amp;&gt;li]:before:self-center">
<li class=""><code>/v1/connectors</code> (Managed connectors for RAG)</li>
<li class=""><code>/v1/chat</code> parameters: <code>connectors</code>, <code>search_queries_only</code></li>
<li class=""><code>/v1/generate</code> (Legacy generative endpoint)</li>
<li class=""><code>/v1/summarize</code> (Legacy summarization endpoint)</li>
<li class=""><code>/v1/classify</code></li>
<li class="">Slack App integration</li>
<li class="">Coral Web UI (chat.cohere.com and coral.cohere.com)</li>
</ul>
<p>For questions, reach out to <a class="fern-mdx-link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" href="mailto:support@cohere.com">support@cohere.com<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" data-fern-style="margin:-1px"><ul><li><a href="/changelog" tabindex="-1" aria-current="true">v2 API</a></li><li><a href="/v1/changelog" tabindex="-1">v1 API</a></li></ul></nav><script>
        (function () {
          // Runs on the initial load AND on every view-transition swap: Astro
          // re-inserts the `transition:persist`ed sidebar into the new document,
          // and re-inserting a scroll container resets its scrollTop to 0, so we
          // restore the position from sessionStorage before paint to avoid a
          // visible jump. The companion scroll listener below keeps that stored
          // value in lockstep with the live scroll (the hydrated build did this
          // via React); without it the restored value drifts stale and the
          // sidebar jumps to the wrong offset on navigation.
          try {
            var KEY = "fern-sidebar-scroll-position";
            var container = document.getElementById("fern-sidebar-scroll-area");
            if (!container) { return; }
            var storedRaw = window.sessionStorage.getItem(KEY);
            var stored = storedRaw != null ? parseInt(storedRaw, 10) : 0;
            if (Number.isFinite(stored) && stored > 0) {
              container.scrollTop = stored;
              return;
            }
            var active = container.querySelector('.fern-sidebar-link[data-state="active"]');
            if (!active) { return; }
            var cRect = container.getBoundingClientRect();
            var aRect = active.getBoundingClientRect();
            var isAbove = aRect.top < cRect.top;
            var isBelow = aRect.bottom > cRect.bottom;
            if (!isAbove && !isBelow) { return; }
            var offset = aRect.top - cRect.top + container.scrollTop;
            var target = Math.max(0, Math.round(offset - container.clientHeight / 3));
            container.scrollTop = target;
            if (target > 0) {
              window.sessionStorage.setItem(KEY, String(target));
            }
          } catch (e) { /* sessionStorage/layout unavailable — fall back to the hydration-time scroll */ }
        })();
      </script></main><!-- Footer (matches AbstractDefaultDocs footer) --><footer id="fern-footer" class="width-before-scroll-bar"></footer></div><div
